{"lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Some people say that a hangover is caused by impurities in the drink, not by the alcohol per se.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Without determination by or involvement of extraneous factors; by its very nature."],"synonyms":[{"word":"by itself"},{"word":"in itself"},{"word":"in and of itself"},{"word":"as such"},{"word":"sui generis"},{"word":"by definition"},{"word":"by nature"},{"word":"essentially"},{"word":"in essence"},{"word":"ipso facto"},{"word":"intrinsically"},{"word":"essentially"},{"word":"in and of itself"},{"word":"inherently"},{"word":"innately"},{"word":"intrinsically"},{"word":"naturally"},{"word":"per se"}],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"It's not a museum per se, but they do have some interesting artefacts.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a true or literal sense; as one would expect from the name or description."],"qualifier":"more loosely","raw_tags":["in the negative"],"synonyms":[{"word":"exactly"},{"word":"strictly speaking"},{"word":"sensu stricto"},{"word":"stricto sensu"},{"word":"truly"}],"tags":["nonstandard","not-comparable"]},{"glosses":["As the principle of its own determination and positing itself."],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["human-sciences","philosophy","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The law makes drunk driving illegal per se.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Not leaving discretion to the judge to take into account additional factors that could rebut the judgment, deriving the qualification from the statute."],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["law"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/pəˈseɪ/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/pɚˈseɪ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/pɚˈsiː/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/pəˈsæɪ/","tags":["Australia"]}],"upstream_line":203163,"upstream_offset":981081769,"upstream_record_sha256":"58d3bd553922faa07d9df43efbe3964bc55f12f4f57464b0df925d726cdacbb1","word":"per se"}
